Outfit suggestion:

```
Here is a practical thrift styling suggestion featuring the Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print.

### Outfit 1: Casual Y2K Streetwear
This look balances the fitted, feminine energy of the baby tee with relaxed, streetwear-inspired bottoms. 

**Owned Pieces Used:**
*   **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**
*   **Black crossbody bag**

**Suggestions (Not Owned):**
*   *Suggestion: Retro platform sandals or pink sneakers to tie into the tee's butterfly print*
*   *Suggestion: Silver chain necklace*

**Why It Works:**
The dark blue, high-waisted baggy jeans anchor the vibrant white, pink, and purple tones of the Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print, creating a classic early 2000s contrast between a fitted top and loose bottoms. The black crossbody bag adds a simple, everyday touch that keeps the focus on the butterfly graphic without overwhelming the outfit's colors.
```

Fit card:

```
Just scored this Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print on Depop for $18.00! I love the early 2000s butterfly graphic against the white, pink, and purple fabric. The listing notes it has a fitted crop length and a tag size medium that fits like a small. I am planning to style it with baggy dark wash straight-leg jeans, retro platform sandals, and a simple black crossbody bag for a classic streetwear look.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
Here are two outfit suggestions featuring the selected **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** and your owned wardrobe pieces.

### Outfit 1: Effortless Y2K Streetwear
* **Selected Listing:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Owned Pieces:** 
  * Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash (w_001)
  * Black cropped zip hoodie (w_005)
  * Chunky white sneakers (w_007)
  * Black crossbody bag (w_010)
* **Suggested Pieces:** Silver chain necklace, small hoop earrings

**Styling Notes:**
This look plays on the classic Y2K proportion-play of a tight top over baggy bottoms. The fitted crop length of the baby tee contrasts nicely with the high-waisted, relaxed fit of the dark wash baggy jeans. The white, pink, and purple in the butterfly graphic tie back easily to the crisp white chunky sneakers, while the black cropped zip hoodie (worn open or layered nearby) and black crossbody bag ground the pastel graphics with a bit of edge. 

***

### Outfit 2: Casual Retro Casual-Chic
* **Selected Listing:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Owned Pieces:** 
  * Wide-leg khaki trousers (w_002)
  * Vintage black denim jacket (w_006)
  * Black combat boots (w_008)
  * Brown leather belt (w_009)
* **Suggested Pieces:** Tortoiseshell sunglasses, minimalist rings

**Styling Notes:**
For a slightly more grounded, retro-casual approach, tuck the baby tee into the wide-leg khaki trousers and accent the waist with the brown leather belt to pull in warm earth tones. Tossing the slightly cropped vintage black denim jacket over top creates a great mix of textures. The pink and purple tones in the butterfly print pop against the neutral khaki trousers, and the black combat boots give the outfit a subtle, grounded finish that balances the sweetness of the tee.
```

      out: "Here are two outfit suggestions featuring the selected **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** and your owned wardrobe pieces.\n\n### Outfit 1: Effortless Y2K Streetwear\n* **Selected Listing:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print\n* **Owned Pieces:** \n  * Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash (w_001)\n  * Black cropped zip hoodie (w_005)\n  * Chunky white sneakers (w_007)\n  * Black crossbody bag (w_010)\n* **Suggested Pieces:** Silver chain necklace, small hoop earrings\n\n**Styling Notes:**\nThis look plays on the classic Y2K proportion-play of a tight top over baggy bottoms. The fitted crop length of the baby tee contrasts nicely with the high-waisted, relaxed fit of the dark wash baggy jeans. The white, pink, and purple in the butterfly graphic tie back easily to the crisp white chunky sneakers, while the black cropped zip hoodie (worn open or layered nearby) and black crossbody bag ground the pastel graphics with a bit of edge. \n\n***\n\n### Outfit 2: Casual Retro Casual-Chic\n* **Selected Listing:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print\n* **Owned Pieces:** \n  * Wide-leg khaki trousers (w_002)\n  * Vintage black denim jacket (w_006)\n  * Black combat boots (w_008)\n  * Brown leather belt (w_009)\n* **Suggested Pieces:** Tortoiseshell sunglasses, minimalist rings\n\n**Styling Notes:**\nFor a slightly more grounded, retro-casual approach, tuck the baby tee into the wide-leg khaki trousers and accent the waist with the brown leather belt to pull in warm earth tones. Tossing the slightly cropped vintage black denim jacket over top creates a great mix of textures. The pink and purple tones in the butterfly print pop against the neutral khaki trousers, and the black combat boots give the outfit a subtle, grounded finish that balances the sweetness of the tee."

```
Here is a practical thrift styling suggestion featuring the Y2K baby tee:

### Outfit: Casual Y2K Streetwear

**Owned pieces used:**
- **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash** (Bottoms)
- **Black cropped zip hoodie** (Tops - layered over or carried)
- **Chunky white sneakers** (Shoes)
- **Black crossbody bag** (Accessories)

**Listing used:**
- Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print

**Suggested extra pieces:**
- *Suggestion:* Silver butterfly hairpin (Accessories)
- *Suggestion:* Thin silver chain necklace (Accessories)

**Why this works:**
This look balances the fitted, cropped silhouette of the Y2K Baby Tee with the relaxed, high-waisted fit of the Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash, capturing a classic early 2000s streetwear contrast. The white in the baby tee ties directly into the chunky white sneakers to create visual bookends, while the dark blue indigo wash of the jeans anchors the pastel pink and purple tones of the butterfly graphic. Adding the black crossbody bag ties in with the dark denim and keeps the accessories minimal and functional.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
Here are two thrift-styled outfit ideas featuring the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print**, balancing its fitted, early 2000s aesthetic with pieces from your wardrobe.

### Outfit 1: Effortless Y2K Streetwear
This look pairs the fitted, graphic-heavy baby tee with relaxed denim and classic footwear for a balanced proportions play.

*   **Top (Selected Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Owned Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Owned Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Owned Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   **Suggestion (Not Owned):** *Silver butterfly hair clips or a thin silver chain necklace*

**Why it works:** 
The fitted silhouette of the baby tee contrasts sharply with the loose, relaxed fit of the baggy dark wash jeans, hitting that classic Y2K streetwear proportion. The white in the chunky white sneakers pulls out the white base of the tee, while the black crossbody bag ties into the graphic's casual, everyday feel.

---

### Outfit 2: Contrast Grunge & Earth Tones
This outfit uses contrasting textures and styles to tone down the sweetness of the butterfly print with tougher, structured elements.

*   **Top (Selected Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Owned Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Owned Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Owned Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Owned Accessories:** Brown leather belt, Black crossbody bag
*   **Suggestion (Not Owned):** *Layered vintage silver rings*

**Why it works:**
The pink, purple, and white butterfly print on the tee pops nicely against the neutral khaki and tan of the wide-leg trousers. Adding the vintage black denim jacket and black combat boots brings in a touch of grunge, grounding the sweet Y2K graphic with heavier, classic outerwear. The brown leather belt adds a warm earth tone accent that bridges the gap between the khaki pants and the dark jacket.
```

```
Here is a practical, styled outfit featuring the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers
* **Accessories (Owned):** Black crossbody bag
* **Suggestion (Not owned):** *Silver butterfly hair clips or a beaded choker necklace to lean into the Y2K aesthetic.*

**How the colors and styles work together:**
* **Proportions:** The fitted, cropped silhouette of the Y2K baby tee balances the voluminous, high-waisted fit of the baggy dark wash jeans, creating that classic early 2000s contrast. 
* **Color Coordination:** The white base of the baby tee ties directly into the chunky white sneakers, creating visual bookends for the outfit. The pink and purple butterfly graphic pops against the dark indigo denim, keeping the look playful yet grounded.
* **Style Pairing:** The retro, graphic-heavy nature of the listing leans into streetwear when paired with the baggy denim and chunky sneakers, while the black crossbody bag adds a clean, minimal finishing touch.
```

```
Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers
* **Accessories (Owned):** Black crossbody bag
* **Suggested Extra Piece:** *Suggestion: Layer a thin silver chain necklace or butterfly hair clips to lean further into the 2000s aesthetic.*

**Why this works:**
* **Proportions:** The fitted, cropped nature of the Y2K Baby Tee creates a balanced silhouette when paired against the voluminous, high-waisted Baggy straight-leg jeans, perfectly capturing an authentic early 2000s streetwear look.
* **Color Coordination:** The white base and pink/purple tones in the butterfly graphic tie directly into the crisp, clean finish of the Chunky white sneakers. 
* **Style Harmony:** The casual streetwear tags of the denim and sneakers ground the playful, vintage energy of the graphic baby tee, while the Black crossbody bag provides a simple, utilitarian everyday finish.
```

      out: "Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:\n\n### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual\n* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print\n* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash\n* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers\n* **Accessories (Owned):** Black crossbody bag\n* **Suggested Extra Piece:** *Suggestion: Layer a thin silver chain necklace or butterfly hair clips to lean further into the 2000s aesthetic.*\n\n**Why this works:**\n* **Proportions:** The fitted, cropped nature of the Y2K Baby Tee creates a balanced silhouette when paired against the voluminous, high-waisted Baggy straight-leg jeans, perfectly capturing an authentic early 2000s streetwear look.\n* **Color Coordination:** The white base and pink/purple tones in the butterfly graphic tie directly into the crisp, clean finish of the Chunky white sneakers. \n* **Style Harmony:** The casual streetwear tags of the denim and sneakers ground the playful, vintage energy of the graphic baby tee, while the Black crossbody bag provides a simple, utilitarian everyday finish."

```
Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Outerwear (Owned):** Vintage black denim jacket
* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers
* **Accessories (Owned):** Black crossbody bag
* **Accessories (Suggestion):** *Silver butterfly hair clips or a beaded choker necklace*

#### Why this works:
* **Proportions:** The fitted, cropped silhouette of the Y2K Baby Tee balances out the relaxed volume of the Baggy straight-leg jeans, creating a classic high-contrast silhouette that defines Y2K styling.
* **Color Coordination:** The white base and pink/purple tones in the butterfly graphic on the baby tee tie directly into the crisp Chunky white sneakers, while the dark blue indigo of the jeans anchors the look. 
* **Style Harmony:** The Vintage black denim jacket layers effortlessly over the tee while leaning into the nostalgic, vintage streetwear aesthetic of the listing.
```

      out: "Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:\n\n### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual\n* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print\n* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash\n* **Outerwear (Owned):** Vintage black denim jacket\n* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers\n* **Accessories (Owned):** Black crossbody bag\n* **Accessories (Suggestion):** *Silver butterfly hair clips or a beaded choker necklace*\n\n#### Why this works:\n* **Proportions:** The fitted, cropped silhouette of the Y2K Baby Tee balances out the relaxed volume of the Baggy straight-leg jeans, creating a classic high-contrast silhouette that defines Y2K styling.\n* **Color Coordination:** The white base and pink/purple tones in the butterfly graphic on the baby tee tie directly into the crisp Chunky white sneakers, while the dark blue indigo of the jeans anchors the look. \n* **Style Harmony:** The Vintage black denim jacket layers effortlessly over the tee while leaning into the nostalgic, vintage streetwear aesthetic of the listing."

```
Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers
* **Accessories & Extras:** 
  * **Owned Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
  * **Suggested Extra:** Silver chain necklace (Suggestion — not owned)

**How it works together:**
This look plays on classic Y2K proportions by contrasting a fitted, cropped top with relaxed, high-waisted bottoms. The white, pink, and purple tones in the butterfly print of the baby tee pop against the deep dark blue (indigo) of the baggy straight-leg jeans. Tying the outfit together, the chunky white sneakers echo the white base of the tee, while the black crossbody bag grounds the look with minimal, everyday functionality.
```

```
Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit using the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Bottoms (Owned):** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Shoes (Owned):** Chunky white sneakers
* **Accessories & Outerwear (Owned):** Black crossbody bag
* **Suggested Extra Pieces:** *Silver chain necklace, thin wire-frame sunglasses*

#### Why this works:
* **Proportions:** The fitted, cropped silhouette of the baby tee balances out the volume of the baggy, high-waisted dark wash jeans, creating a classic Y2K contrast between tight and loose. 
* **Color Palette:** The white, pink, and purple in the butterfly graphic pop against the deep indigo of the dark wash denim. Tying the look together, the chunky white sneakers echo the white base of the tee, keeping the color story cohesive and intentional.
```

The listing notes it has a fitted crop length and that the tag says medium but fits like a small.

Here is a practical thrift styling suggestion featuring the Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Contrast

**Owned Pieces Used:**
*   **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash** (Bottoms)
*   **Black combat boots** (Shoes)
*   **Black crossbody bag** (Accessories)

**Suggested Pieces (Not owned):**
*   *Suggestion: Silver chain necklace*
*   *Suggestion: Frameless tinted sunglasses*

**Why it works:**
This look plays on the contrast between tight and loose proportions. The listing is a fitted, cropped Y2K baby tee featuring white, pink, and purple tones with a butterfly graphic. Pairing it with the dark blue, high-waisted baggy straight-leg jeans creates an authentic early 2000s streetwear silhouette, balancing the ultra-feminine, fitted top with relaxed, heavy-wash denim. The black combat boots anchor the outfit with a tougher edge that complements the black crossbody bag, tying the accessories together while letting the pink and purple hues of the butterfly print pop against the dark wash.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
Here are two practical thrift-styled outfits utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces.

### Outfit 1: Casual Y2K Streetwear
**Owned pieces used:**
*   Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash (w_001)
*   Chunky white sneakers (w_007)
*   Black crossbody bag (w_010)

**Listing used:**
*   Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print (lst_002)

**Suggested extra pieces:**
*   *Suggestion:* Silver chain necklace
*   *Suggestion:* Thin frameless sunglasses

**How it works together:**
This look plays on proportions by pairing the fitted crop length of the butterfly-print baby tee with the high-waisted, relaxed fit of the dark wash baggy straight-leg jeans. The white in the baby tee ties directly into the chunky white sneakers for a cohesive color balance, while the black crossbody bag matches the dark tones of the denim and adds everyday utility. 

***

### Outfit 2: Edgy Contrast Look
**Owned pieces used:**
*   Wide-leg khaki trousers (w_002)
*   Vintage black denim jacket (w_006)
*   Black combat boots (w_008)
*   Brown leather belt (w_009)

**Listing used:**
*   Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print (lst_002)

**Suggested extra pieces:**
*   *Suggestion:* Silver hoop earrings

**How it works together:**
This outfit juxtaposes the sweet, colorful cottagecore elements of the baby tee (featuring pink, purple, and white graphics) with rugged, utilitarian pieces. Tucking the tee into the wide-leg khaki trousers and accenting it with the brown leather belt grounds the outfit in earthy tones. Layering the slightly cropped vintage black denim jacket on top and finishing the look with black combat boots adds a sharp edge that contrasts nicely with the softer pastel tones of the butterfly print.
```

```
Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
* **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
* **Suggested Extra Piece:** *Suggestion:* Pastel pink claw clip or thin silver hoop earrings to lean into the early 2000s theme.

#### Why It Works:
* **Color Coordination:** The white, pink, and purple tones in the butterfly graphic of the baby tee tie in effortlessly with the crisp white of the **Chunky white sneakers**. The rich, dark blue indigo of the **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash** grounds the pastel colors of the graphic tee so the outfit doesn't feel overly sweet.
* **Proportion & Style:** The fitted, cropped silhouette of the Y2K Baby Tee creates a balanced, classic 2000s silhouette when paired against the relaxed, loose fit of the high-waisted **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Finishing the look with the **Black crossbody bag** and **Chunky white sneakers** ties the streetwear elements together.
```

```
Here is a practical, thrift-styled outfit utilizing the selected listing and your owned wardrobe pieces:

### Outfit: Y2K Streetwear Casual
* **Top (Selected Listing):** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
* **Owned Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
* **Owned Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
* **Owned Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
* **Suggested Extra Piece:** *Silver hoop earrings*

**Why this works together:**
The fitted, cropped silhouette of the Y2K Baby Tee contrasts sharply with the relaxed volume of the Baggy straight-leg jeans, creating a balanced Y2K-inspired streetwear proportion. Color-wise, the white base and subtle pink and purple tones in the butterfly graphic are neatly echoed and grounded by the Chunky white sneakers, tying the footwear and top together. Finally, the Black crossbody bag and *suggested silver hoop earrings* add practical, minimal accessories that lean into the 2000s aesthetic without competing with the graphic tee.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
Here is a practical thrift styling suggestion featuring the Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print:

### Outfit: Casual Y2K Streetwear

**Owned pieces used:**
* **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash** (w_001)
* **Black crossbody bag** (w_010)

**Listing used:**
* Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print (lst_002)

**Suggested (not owned) pieces:**
* Suggestion: Retro pink-tinted sunglasses
* Suggestion: White platform sandals

**Why it works:**
This outfit balances proportions and plays into the Y2K aesthetic of the baby tee. The fitted, cropped silhouette of the butterfly tee contrasts nicely with the relaxed, high-waisted fit of the dark wash baggy jeans. Color-wise, the white, pink, and purple tones in the tee's graphic pop against the deep indigo of the denim, tying the look together effortlessly while keeping the focus on the vintage print.
```

```

      "returned": "Here are two outfit suggestions featuring the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print**, styled using your owned wardrobe pieces and a couple of suggested additions to complete the look.\n\n### Outfit 1: Effortless Streetwear Y2K\n* **Owned Pieces:** \n  * Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash\n  * Vintage black denim jacket\n  * Chunky white sneakers\n  * Black crossbody bag\n* **Suggested Additions:** \n  * Silver butterfly hairpin (Suggestion - Not owned)\n\n**Why it works:** \nThis look plays on proportions by pairing the fitted, cropped nature of the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with the relaxed, high-waisted fit of the **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. The white, pink, and purple tones in the butterfly graphic are balanced by the deep indigo of the denim. Throwing on the **Vintage black denim jacket** adds a classic, slightly cropped layer that complements the streetwear vibe, while the **Chunky white sneakers** tie in the white base of the baby tee. Finally, the **Black crossbody bag** echoes the dark outerwear to keep the color palette cohesive.\n\n***\n\n### Outfit 2: Casual Earth-Tone Contrast\n* **Owned Pieces:** \n  * Wide-leg khaki trousers\n  * Brown leather belt\n  * Black combat boots\n  * Black crossbody bag\n* **Suggested Additions:** \n  * Delicate gold layered necklace (Suggestion - Not owned)\n\n**Why it works:** \nThis outfit mixes the playful, graphic aesthetic of the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with tailored, utilitarian basics. The warm tan and khaki tones of the pants create an earthy background that makes the pink and purple hues in the butterfly print pop. Tucking the tee in and adding the **Brown leather belt** pulls the waist together, while the **Black combat boots** and **Black crossbody bag** add an edgy, grounding contrast to the softer pastel colors of the top."

Outfit suggestion:

```
Here are two outfit suggestions featuring the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print**, styled using your owned wardrobe pieces and a couple of suggested additions to complete the look.

### Outfit 1: Effortless Streetwear Y2K
* **Owned Pieces:** 
  * Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
  * Vintage black denim jacket
  * Chunky white sneakers
  * Black crossbody bag
* **Suggested Additions:** 
  * Silver butterfly hairpin (Suggestion - Not owned)

**Why it works:** 
This look plays on proportions by pairing the fitted, cropped nature of the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with the relaxed, high-waisted fit of the **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. The white, pink, and purple tones in the butterfly graphic are balanced by the deep indigo of the denim. Throwing on the **Vintage black denim jacket** adds a classic, slightly cropped layer that complements the streetwear vibe, while the **Chunky white sneakers** tie in the white base of the baby tee. Finally, the **Black crossbody bag** echoes the dark outerwear to keep the color palette cohesive.

***

### Outfit 2: Casual Earth-Tone Contrast
* **Owned Pieces:** 
  * Wide-leg khaki trousers
  * Brown leather belt
  * Black combat boots
  * Black crossbody bag
* **Suggested Additions:** 
  * Delicate gold layered necklace (Suggestion - Not owned)

**Why it works:** 
This outfit mixes the playful, graphic aesthetic of the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with tailored, utilitarian basics. The fitted cut of the baby tee contrasts sharply with the roomy shape of the **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. The warm tan and khaki tones of the pants create an earthy background that makes the pink and purple hues in the butterfly print pop. Tucking the tee in and adding the **Brown leather belt** pulls the waist together, while the **Black combat boots** and **Black crossbody bag** add an edgy, grounding contrast to the softer pastel colors of the top.
```

  "outfit": "Here are two outfit suggestions featuring the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print**, styled using your owned wardrobe pieces and a couple of suggested additions to complete the look.\n\n### Outfit 1: Effortless Streetwear Y2K\n* **Owned Pieces:** \n  * Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash\n  * Vintage black denim jacket\n  * Chunky white sneakers\n  * Black crossbody bag\n* **Suggested Additions:** \n  * Silver butterfly hairpin (Suggestion - Not owned)\n\n**Why it works:** \nThis look plays on proportions by pairing the fitted, cropped nature of the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with the relaxed, high-waisted fit of the **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. The white, pink, and purple tones in the butterfly graphic are balanced by the deep indigo of the denim. Throwing on the **Vintage black denim jacket** adds a classic, slightly cropped layer that complements the streetwear vibe, while the **Chunky white sneakers** tie in the white base of the baby tee. Finally, the **Black crossbody bag** echoes the dark outerwear to keep the color palette cohesive.\n\n***\n\n### Outfit 2: Casual Earth-Tone Contrast\n* **Owned Pieces:** \n  * Wide-leg khaki trousers\n  * Brown leather belt\n  * Black combat boots\n  * Black crossbody bag\n* **Suggested Additions:** \n  * Delicate gold layered necklace (Suggestion - Not owned)\n\n**Why it works:** \nThis outfit mixes the playful, graphic aesthetic of the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with tailored, utilitarian basics. The fitted cut of the baby tee contrasts sharply with the roomy shape of the **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. The warm tan and khaki tones of the pants create an earthy background that makes the pink and purple hues in the butterfly print pop. Tucking the tee in and adding the **Brown leather belt** pulls the waist together, while the **Black combat boots** and **Black crossbody bag** add an edgy, grounding contrast to the softer pastel colors of the top.",
